private def outputValue (value : Core.Value) : Except String String := do
  let value ← CoreExpr.value value
  return String.fromUTF8! (ByteArray.mk (SQLite.Text.bytes value))
def main : IO Unit := do
  for group in [cases0,cases200,cases400,cases600,cases800,cases1000,cases1200,cases1400] do
    for (i,sql) in group do
      let actual : Except String String := do
        let (_, results) ← CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty sql
        let lines ← results.flatMap (·.rows) |>.mapM fun row => do
          pure (String.intercalate "|" (← row.mapM outputValue) ++ "\n")
        pure (SQLite.Text.hexBytes (String.join lines).toUTF8.data)
      let text := match actual with
        | .ok output => output
        | .error message => "error:" ++ SQLite.Text.hexBytes (SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes message)
      IO.println s!"{i}|{text}"
